Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

中文摘要翻译: 基于事件的时间图神经网络（ETGNNs）已在包括社交网络分析、流行病追踪、推荐系统和政治事件预测等广泛应用中展现出强大的性能。然而，其日益增长的复杂性给可解释性带来了重大挑战。现有的解释方法仅关注ETGNN内部信息流的子集，通常仅追踪从事件相关嵌入到输出的贡献。因此，它们忽视了通过事件诱导变量的重要通路，这些变量调解了节点之间的交互，从而在捕获长程时间依赖中发挥核心作用。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种新颖的归因方法，分析所有事件相关变量的完整信息流。我们的方法基于最新的归一化相关测度（NRM）框架，能够显式量化源自事件嵌入的信息流以及通过事件诱导变量的信息流。该方法还确保了跨层潜变量的可比性，并支持事件间交互的高阶分析。为应对ETGNN的架构复杂性，我们扩展了NRM框架，引入了一种模块化解构流程，便于为复杂神经架构系统性地构建关联结构。我们在两个用于流行病追踪和社会动态的合成数据集以及一个真实世界的政治事件网络数据集上评估了我们的方法。定性和定量实验表明，我们的方法一致优于现有的解释方法，同时生成更符合人类可解释性的解释。

</details>


### [177] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了图神经网络（GNN）的研究现状，围绕统一的设计空间组织了光谱与空间两大范式的第一性原理推导，将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Lehman层次联系起来，并跨越12个应用领域系统分析了图结构何时值得计算成本、何时不应使用，揭示了跨领域的共性模式与挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着图神经网络成为处理关系数据的默认模型类别，核心问题已不再是“消息传递是否有帮助”，而是“图结构在哪里值得计算成本、在哪里不值得”。本文旨在通过统一的设计空间框架，系统地梳理GNN的方法、表达能力和应用，帮助研究者做出更明智的模型选择。

Method: 本文采用综述方法：首先构建单一设计空间，从共享第一性原理推导光谱与空间两种图神经网络公式；然后将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Lehman层次相连接，明确当前架构能分离和不能分离的内容；最后对12个应用领域分别指定图构建选择及其成本，识别主导架构及其原因，并将报告的性能提升从弱基线或有利数据划分产生的人为假象中分离出来。

Result: 跨领域比较揭示了若干重复出现的模式：异配性和规模问题几乎在所有领域都会削弱相同模型的效果；时序图始终比静态图更具挑战性；在公共排行榜上排名靠前的架构很少是实际部署的架构。此外，过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性等被视为决定模型采纳的关键约束。

Conclusion: 图神经网络的领域已发展到需要在统一框架下审视其设计空间和成本效益的阶段。异构性和规模、时序动态性、以及从学术基准到实际部署之间的差距，是当前GNN面临的共性核心挑战。过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、公平性和可解释性等问题并非可有可无的检查清单，而是决定模型能否被采纳的关键约束条件。

Original Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

中文摘要翻译: 图神经网络已经从一种小众的表示学习技术发展成为处理关系型数据的默认模型类别。有趣的问题不再是消息传递是否在给定数据集上有所帮助，而是图结构在哪些场景下值得其计算成本、在哪些场景下不值得。本综述围绕一个单一设计空间组织该领域，从共享第一性原理推导出光谱与空间两种公式，并将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次联系起来，明确陈述了当前架构能够分离和不能分离的内容。以此方法论为基础，我们考察了12个应用领域，包括推荐系统与社交网络、知识图谱与语言模型集成、药物发现与分子性质学习、医疗健康与神经科学、计算机视觉、交通与城市计算、电力与可再生能源系统、无线与第六代网络、欺诈检测与网络安全、工业预测、材料科学以及气候建模。针对每个领域，我们指定了图构建选择及其成本，识别了哪些架构家族占主导地位及其原因，并将报告的性能提升从弱基线或有利数据划分产生的人为假象中分离出来。跨领域比较揭示了重复出现的模式：异配性和规模几乎在所有领域都会削弱相同的模型；时序图始终比静态图更具挑战性；在公共排行榜上排名靠前的架构很少是实际部署的架构。我们将过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性处理为并非可有可无的收官检查清单，而是决定模型是否被采纳的关键约束条件。

</details>


### [178] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对共享词汇表的LLM家族（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中无训练源选择问题，提出FisherSketch方法，通过单次流式计算头Fisher对齐的余弦相似度（激活-误差联合空间中的核均值嵌入），避免了传统表示相似度度量的不可识别性和经典几何度量的计算爆炸，实现了轻量级（16KB任务签名+192KB流状态）且具迁移信息量的源选择。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在科学字符串领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列），候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同，形成"激活-暗"机制：表示相似度度量在缺乏标签条件误差几何假设时无信息量，而经典更新几何度量在词汇表规模下计算不可行。需要一种既高效又能捕获迁移相关更新结构的源选择方法。

Method: FisherSketch方法。核心发现：头Fisher对齐恰好等于激活-误差联合空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦相似度，暴露出激活因子、误差因子和耦合因子，无需具体化Fisher矩阵。该方法通过单次流式传递直接估计该余弦值，生成极小的任务签名（16KB，m=4096）和每任务流状态（192KB），可存储于模型哈希旁。

Result: 1) 在共享输出头设置下，表示度量（如CKA）对迁移是不可识别的：模型可共享相同表示却拥有正交的头更新；2) FisherSketch使K=128,256头Fisher对齐变得实用；3) 在Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer偏移实验中，当激活相似度无法区分任务时，FisherSketch仍然具有信息量；4) 相同的签名和边缘分布可作为诊断工具，研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动。

Conclusion: 提出了一种轻量级、可流式计算的头Fisher对齐估计方法，解决了共享词汇表LLM家族中源选择的关键瓶颈，同时为理解LLM任务相似性的内在驱动因素提供了诊断工具。

Original Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 针对具有共享词汇表的LLM家族的无训练源选择问题，出现在SMILES、蛋白质和基因组序列等科学字符串领域中，这些领域的候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同。这产生了一种"激活-暗"机制：表示相似度度量在缺乏标签条件误差几何假设时可能无信息量，而经典的更新几何度量在词汇表规模下计算不可行。我们表明，在共享输出头设置中，表示度量（如CKA）对迁移是不可识别的：模型可以共享完全相同的表示却拥有正交的头更新。关键恒等式是：头Fisher对齐恰好是激活-误差联合空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦相似度，从而暴露出激活因子、误差因子和耦合因子，而无需具体化Fisher矩阵。FisherSketch通过单次流式传递直接估计该余弦值，使得K=128,256头Fisher对齐变得实用，仅需16KB的任务签名（m=4096）和192KB的每任务流状态，小到可以存储在模型哈希旁，却能编码与迁移相关的更新结构。除源选择外，相同的签名和边缘分布还提供了诊断工具，用于研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动；共享参数和内部层验证，以及Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer偏移实验表明，当激活相似度无法区分任务时，FisherSketch仍然具有信息量。

</details>


### [179] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，将线性生成器的学习扩展到具有类别依赖、相关且非零均值的潜变量结构，并证明微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由有效协方差驱动的确定性常微分方程，揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关可提升弱方向的可学习性，而过强的相关会破坏恢复稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可解GAN分析假设无条件信号且潜变量协方差为对角矩阵，无法处理类别依赖、相关和非零均值的复杂潜变量结构。本文旨在拓展这一框架，研究这些异质性结构对GAN训练动力学的影响。

Method: 构建线性生成器与二次能量判别器的可解高维GAN模型，引入概率加权有效二阶矩刻画潜变量异质性。在高维极限下证明微观随机训练过程收敛到确定性常微分方程，并对匹配协方差特例进行模态级稳定性分析。

Result: 理论推导出学习率与噪声水平决定的可学习区间：当领先有效特征值越过下阈值时学习开始，所有相关有效模态保持在区间内时实现完全恢复。揭示了信号增强机制——低秩相关可提升弱方向，过强相关则破坏稳定性。数值模拟验证了ODE、相边界和增强机制，真实数据集实验表明信息性生成器协方差改善了与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐。

Conclusion: 潜变量结构的异质性通过有效协方差统一影响GAN训练动力学；信号增强机制表明适当的相关性有助于弱模式学习，但过强相关有害；生成器协方差的先验信息可提升子空间学习性能。

Original Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。先前的可解GAN分析假设具有对角潜协方差的无条件信号；我们将多特征判别器设置扩展到类别依赖、相关且非零均值的潜变量结构。对于二次能量判别器，所有此类异质性通过概率加权有效二阶矩进入动力学过程。我们证明，在高维极限下，随机微观训练过程收敛到由该有效协方差驱动的确定性常微分方程。在匹配协方差特例中，稳定性分析给出了由学习率和噪声水平确定的模态级可解区间：当领先有效特征值跨越下阈值时学习开始，而完全恢复要求所有相关有效模态保持在区间内。这揭示了一种信号增强机制：低秩相关可以提升弱方向越过可学习性阈值，而过强的相关则会破坏恢复的稳定性。数值模拟验证了ODE、相边界和增强机制。在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验进一步表明，信息性生成器协方差改善了与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐。

</details>


### [180] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，涵盖情绪障碍（焦虑和抑郁）、关系压力源触发因素及关系阶段信息。在此基础上，对七种微调Transformer模型和五种大语言模型在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务上进行基准测试，发现模型家族间存在明显的任务依赖性差异，并揭示焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间的强关联。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康研究通常将心理疾病视为孤立现象，忽略了人际背景（如亲密关系）对心理困扰的影响。作者希望引入关系上下文，从长距离关系的Reddit帖子中同时捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的触发因素。

Method: 构建RSPC语料库（1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子），覆盖诊断类别（焦虑、抑郁）、关系压力源触发因素和关系阶段。对七种微调Transformer模型和五种大语言模型进行多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测三项任务的基准测试。

Result: 模型家族间存在任务依赖性差异：Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上表现最优（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务上表现最强（Macro-F1=0.519）。还发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系上下文的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从以个体为中心向上下文感知的心理健康建模转变，能够捕捉困扰的社会和时间动态。

Original Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

中文摘要翻译: 在自然语言处理中，心理健康状况通常被建模为孤立的现象，而忽略人际背景。我们利用关于异地恋的Reddit帖子来同时捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的关系触发因素。我们引入了关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），该语料库包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，涵盖最普遍的情绪障碍（焦虑和抑郁）、关系压力源触发因素以及关系阶段的指示信息。我们在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务上对七种微调Transformer模型和五种大语言模型进行了基准测试。我们发现模型家族之间存在明显的任务依赖性差异，其中Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上取得了最佳性能（Macro-F1=0.538），而GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务上表现最强（Macro-F1=0.519），这表明不同模型具有不同的能力。我们进一步发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。总体而言，RSPC为考虑关系上下文的NLP任务建立了基准，并支持从以个体为中心向上下文感知的心理健康建模转变，从而能够捕捉困扰的社会和时间动态。

</details>


### [181] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对社交媒体上博彩广告的操纵性和欺骗性检测问题，构建了一个来自Instagram和Reddit的带有人工标注和解释的新数据集，分析了常见说服策略及其对心理健康的影响，并提出了可用于浏览器插件和网络爬虫的自动化检测框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 近年来社交媒体上博彩广告激增，这些广告常使用误导性说服技巧，可能鼓励高风险行为并影响用户心理健康。然而，由于缺乏公开的标注数据集，针对操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的自动化检测研究十分有限。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个社交平台收集博彩相关广告，进行人工标注（包括操纵性和欺骗性分类标签及人类解释），分析常见说服策略及其对心理健康的影响，并构建可解释的自动化检测框架。

Result: 构建了一个带有人工标注和解释说明的博彩广告数据集；分析了博彩广告中常用的说服策略及其对心理健康的潜在影响；提出了可用于浏览器插件和自动化网络爬虫的检测框架。

Conclusion: 该数据集和框架填补了博彩广告欺骗性检测领域的空白，支持可解释的检测方法研究，并可用于开发浏览器插件提醒用户或帮助监管机构自动监控网络博彩广告。

Original Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，博彩应用在社交媒体平台上的推广显著增加。许多此类广告使用可能误导用户、鼓励高风险行为并潜在影响用户心理健康的说服技巧。然而，由于缺乏公开可用的标注数据集，针对操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的自动化检测研究仍然有限。在这项工作中，我们引入了一个从两个广泛使用的社交媒体平台——Instagram和Reddit——收集的博彩广告新数据集。这些广告经过人工标注，标明了操纵性和欺骗性广告行为。除分类标签外，该数据集还包含人工提供的解释，描述了每条标注背后的推理过程，从而支持对可解释的操纵性广告检测方法的研究。此外，我们分析了博彩广告中常用的策略，并研究了这些说服技巧如何影响用户的心理健康。所提出的框架还可以支持实际应用，例如向用户警示操纵性博彩广告的浏览器插件，以及帮助监管机构在线监控和检测此类促销活动的自动化网络爬虫。

</details>


### [182] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了时间序列预测中“更大模型带来更高精度”的主流假设，通过Ridge回归实验发现，优化预处理策略（如上下文长度、归一化方式和正则化）可以在远低于扩展模型成本的情况下，接近甚至超越大型模型的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究倾向于使用越来越大的架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型），认为模型容量是提升精度的关键。作者持相反观点，认为通过调整预处理策略可以以更低成本缩小性能差距。

Method: 以Ridge回归为测试平台（因其具有闭式解和可解释权重），在八个标准基准上搜索超参数，包括上下文长度、局部归一化（学习上下文末尾的滑动窗口比例而非整体）、正则化和数据增强。

Result: (1) 最优回溯长度具有强序列特异性，且与预测窗口呈非单调关系，幂律指数从+0.46到-0.19不等，挑战了“更长预测需要更长历史”的惯例。(2) 使用上下文尾部滑动窗口进行归一化几乎总是更优。(3) 同一数据集内的不同序列通常偏好不同超参数，跨序列共享的最优程度从完全共享到完全独立不等。优化后的模型在大多数数据集-预测窗口组合上超越了线性预测器，并在8个基准中的6个上超过Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。

Conclusion: 时间序列预测中，精心调优的预处理（特别是上下文长度和归一化策略）可以以极低代价获得与大型模型相当的性能。优化的超参数还可作为数据诊断工具，揭示大型模型隐式吸收的数据结构。

Original Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测研究一直在稳步走向更大的架构——从专门的Transformer到通用基础模型——其假设是容量是提升精度的关键。我们持相反立场：通过调整预处理而非扩展模型，可以以更低的成本缩小大部分性能差距。我们以Ridge回归作为测试平台，因为它具有闭式解和可解释的权重，可以直接从搜索中读取最优超参数。我们在八个标准基准上搜索了上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强，发现了三种模式。(1) 最优回溯长度具有很强的序列特异性，且通常与预测窗口呈非单调关系，拟合的幂律指数从ETTm2上的+0.46到Exchange和Traffic上的-0.19不等，挑战了“预测窗口越长需要的历史窗口越长”这一惯例。(2) 在上下文的一个学习到的尾部比例上而非整体上进行归一化，几乎总是更优的选择。(3) 同一数据集内的不同序列常在超参数上存在分歧；跨序列共享的最优程度从完全共享到完全独立不等。由此产生的模型在大多数数据集-预测窗口组合上超越了先前的线性预测器，并在八个基准中的六个上超过了Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。优化后的超参数还可作为数据本身的诊断工具，揭示大型模型默默吸收到其学习参数中的结构。

</details>


### [183] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对从观测数据中学习控制方程（ODE）的理论可辨识性和样本复杂度问题，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了线性到非线性Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场ODE类的可辨识性界，并导出了样本复杂度上界，定量刻画了可靠恢复控制方程所需的观测解数量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有科学机器学习方法虽能从数据中发现控制方程，但缺乏严格的理论条件说明何时能从多个解观测中唯一且稳定地识别出真实的ODE，且文献中对此类学习任务的样本复杂度缺乏定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为微分方程的比较度量，捕获不同方程在所有可容许初始条件下的最坏情况分离程度，编码辨识问题的极小极大结构；基于该度量推导ODE类的度量熵估计，进而分析样本复杂度界。

Result: 建立了线性ODE到具有Lipschitz（Hölder）连续向量场的非线性ODE类的可辨识性界，精确刻画了两个不同方程能从解数据中区分开的条件；导出了相关ODE类的度量熵估计和样本复杂度上界，量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的观测解数量。

Conclusion: 本文为从数据中学习控制方程提供了严格的理论基础，填补了可辨识性和样本复杂度分析方面的空白，Hausdorff距离被证明是分析微分方程辨识问题的有效度量工具。

Original Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

中文摘要翻译: 从观测到的解数据中学习控制方程是科学机器学习中的一个基础性挑战，然而，关于在何种理论条件下可以从多个解观测中唯一且稳定地识别出真实常微分方程的研究尚不成熟，且文献中对此类学习任务的样本复杂度缺乏定量分析。为填补这一空白，我们引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，因为它捕获了所有可容许初始条件下两个方程之间的最坏情况分离程度，从而编码了辨识问题的极小极大结构。我们建立了一类广泛的结构方程——从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz（Hölder）连续向量场的非线性类——中控制ODE的可辨识性界，精确刻画了两个不同方程何时可以从解数据中被区分出来。利用该度量，我们推导了相关ODE类的度量熵估计并分析了样本复杂度上界，量化了需要多少解观测才能可靠地恢复控制方程。

</details>


### [184] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RLAIF的端到端框架，用于生成可移植的求职搜索查询，并深入研究了在对抗性奖励环境下不同优化器对奖励操纵的敏感性，揭示了奖励塑形比优化器选择更为关键。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 求职平台使用低带宽查询接口，难以捕获候选人资料的高维复杂性。现有方法生成的查询往往依赖特定标识符，不具备可移植性和泛化性，且在该任务中奖励信号极易被策略优化所利用，导致退化的逐字复制行为。

Method: 提出了一个端到端的RLAIF（从AI反馈中强化学习）框架，用于生成可移植的求职搜索查询。通过系统的实证实验，隔离优化机制与结构化奖励工程的影响，并引入基于规则的确定性奖励下限来纠正逐字复制带来的错误奖励信号。

Result: 1) 对于无评论家优化器，性能主要取决于稳健的奖励塑形，具体算法选择影响甚微；2) 单次 rollout 基线方法（RLOO和REINFORCE++）天然抵抗奖励操纵，而GRPO的组相对优势归一化使其对虚假奖励信号异常敏感；3) 引入奖励下限可缓解失效模式，在跨族评估器上质量提升+0.147；4) 训练时的奖励模型将性能增益夸大了2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功从根本上依赖于执行奖励塑形纪律，而非选择不同的优化器。奖励工程是决定性能的关键因素。

Original Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

中文摘要翻译: 求职搜索平台依赖低带宽查询接口，这些接口往往难以捕捉候选人资料的高维复杂性。我们提出了一个端到端的RLAIF（基于AI反馈的强化学习）框架，用于生成可移植的求职搜索查询——即抽象掉求职者特定标识符、同时保留可泛化资质的术语。该任务引入了一个高度对抗性的奖励面，策略优化经常利用大语言模型作为评判者的评分标准中的缺陷，导致退化的逐字复制行为。我们进行了全面的实证实验，以隔离优化机制与结构化奖励工程的影响。结果表明，对于无评论家优化器，性能几乎完全由稳健的奖励塑形决定，具体的算法选择基本无关紧要。虽然基于单次rollout基线的无评论家方法（RLOO和REINFORCE++）天然抵抗奖励操纵，但GRPO中的组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号异常敏感，使其极易被利用。我们表明，引入一个确定性的、基于规则的奖励下限来纠正逐字复制分配的奖励，可以缓解这种失效模式，在跨族评估器上带来+0.147的显著质量提升。最终，我们表明训练时的奖励模型将性能增益夸大了2.4倍，从而证实训练成功从根本上依赖于执行奖励塑形纪律，而非选择替代优化器。

</details>


### [185] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多保真度迁移学习框架，结合轻量物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习和少量实验数据，实现板状结构中损伤的精确定位与尺寸估计，以解决标注实验数据稀缺和仿真数据生成成本高的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于导波的结构健康监测中，深度学习模型的实际部署常受限于标注实验数据稀缺以及大规模高保真仿真数据集生成的计算成本过高。

Method: 采用一维时域谱元模型生成大规模合成数据集进行预训练，结合卷积自编码器进行深度特征学习，并通过迁移学习仅用少量标注实验数据将模型适应到实验域，最后使用前馈神经网络进行损伤定位与尺寸估计。

Result: 损伤定位R²得分超过0.93，损伤尺寸估计R²得分超过0.99；在未见过的数据上展现出高预测精度；基于CAE的迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于基于CNN的对应方法。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种准确、计算高效且在实际GWSHM应用中可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

中文摘要翻译: 基于导波的结构健康监测（GWSHM）利用机载换能器对工程结构的早期损伤诊断具有显著潜力。然而，深度学习模型的实际部署常受限于标注实验数据的稀缺以及大规模高保真仿真数据集生成的高昂计算成本。本研究提出了一种多保真度迁移学习框架，该框架融合了轻量级物理仿真、基于卷积自编码器（CAE）的深度特征学习、前馈神经网络以及有限的实验测量，用于压电换能器仪器化板状结构的精确定位与损伤尺寸估计。采用计算高效的一维时域谱元模型生成大规模合成数据集进行预训练，而迁移学习仅用少量标注数据即可使模型适应实验域。基于CAE的迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于基于CNN的对应方法。该模型在损伤定位方面R²得分超过0.93，在损伤尺寸估计方面超过0.99，展现出优异的预测性能。其在未见过的数据上的泛化能力也得到了验证，对预训练或微调期间未出现的损伤场景仍具有高预测精度。研究结果确立了所提出的框架作为一种准确、计算高效且在实际GWSHM应用中切实可行的解决方案。

</details>


### [186] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度均衡（GEQ）与Blackwell可接近性在算法意义上是等价的，进而表明GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等经典在线学习框架也是等价的，并建立了高效归约方法以传递优化与强自适应性等精细保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GEQ是一种新的在线优化框架，与遗憾最小化等经典在线学习框架存在相似性，但此前研究表明GEQ误差与遗憾是不可比较的目标，因此需要明确GEQ在更广泛的在线学习领域中的准确定位。

Method: 通过构造高效归约，证明Blackwell可接近性问题可以用黑盒GEQ oracle求解且无渐近误差损失，反之亦然；进而利用可接近性与遗憾最小化、校准之间的已知等价关系，推导出GEQ与这些框架的等价性。

Result: 建立了GEQ与Blackwell可接近性之间的双向高效归约，表明GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等框架等价；同时识别了GEQ的充分必要条件，并建立了无约束与有约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的归约。

Conclusion: GEQ在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可接近性，进而等价于遗憾最小化和校准等经典在线学习框架；这些归约可用于将优化和强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移到GEQ。

Original Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

中文摘要翻译: 梯度均衡（GEQ）是最近提出的一种在线优化框架，它将离线优化中的一阶平稳性进行推广，并抽象了在线共形预测等问题。虽然GEQ与已知的在线学习框架（如遗憾最小化）存在奇妙的相似性，但先前的研究表明GEQ误差与遗憾是不可比较的目标，因此对于GEQ如何融入更广泛的在线学习领域仍缺乏精确的理解。在本文中，我们证明GEQ在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可接近性。也就是说，Blackwell可接近性问题总可以通过查询黑盒GEQ oracle来求解，且不会在oracle的误差率上产生渐近损失，反之亦然。结合可接近性、遗憾最小化和校准之间已知的等价关系，这些结果表明GEQ也与这些框架等价。我们的归约是高效的，并且可用于将精细保证（如优化和强自适应性）从遗憾最小化迁移到GEQ。在此过程中，我们还识别了GEQ的充分必要条件，并建立了无约束和有约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的归约。

</details>


### [187] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种与Top-k SAE架构兼容的稀疏正则化方法（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚），在不牺牲重构质量的前提下提升了特征的单义性，证明了硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化是互补的。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Top-k SAE虽然通过架构强制稀疏避免了ℓ1惩罚的缺点，但存在固定预算k无法适应输入复杂度、易过拟合到训练k值等局限。作者希望在不放弃架构稀疏优势的同时，引入稀疏正则化来弥补这些不足。

Method: 在Top-k选择前的激活上施加两种正则化：(1) 对未被Top-k选中的单元施加ℓ1惩罚；(2) 施加尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚以集中代码到更少的有效单元。两种惩罚仅作用于batch内至少被Top-k选中过一次的单元。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型和多种k值下，两种正则化器一致地提升了单义性且不损失重构质量。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中到更少的潜在单元，使重构对推理时k值选择更鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探测性能。

Conclusion: 硬性架构稀疏与软性稀疏正则化是互补而非互斥的，可以结合使用以获得更好的单义性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）已成为解释视觉基础模型表征的主流工具，将其多义性激活分解为一组更大规模的稀疏、更单义的特征。Top-k SAE作为当前的标准变体，通过其激活函数在架构层面强制稀疏性，仅保留每个输入中最为活跃的k个潜在单元。由于该设计正是为了避免早期SAE使用的ℓ1惩罚及其已知缺陷，因此尽管Top-k SAE自身存在局限性（如固定的预算k无法适应输入复杂度、倾向于过拟合到训练时使用的k值），它一直没有与显式的稀疏正则化器相结合。我们引入了两种与Top-k架构兼容的稀疏正则化器，二者均作用于Top-k选择之前的激活：一种是对未选中单元的ℓ1惩罚，另一种是尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚，可将代码集中到更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅应用于批次激活单元，即那些在batch内至少被Top-k算子选中一次的单元。在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型以及多种k值下，两种正则化器均在不损失重构质量的前提下一致地提升了单义性。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中到更少的潜在单元，使重构对推理时k值的选择更加鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探测的性能。我们的核心发现是：硬性架构稀疏与软性稀疏正则化是互补而非互斥的。

</details>


### [188] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现世界模型中的幻觉本质上源于状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，并提出了基于数据覆盖率的检测与缓解方法。通过构建MMBench2数据集（427小时、210任务），训练350M参数世界模型，识别出三种幻觉模式（感知、动作边缘化、场景发散），并开发了相应的预测信号。利用覆盖率感知采样和基于好奇心奖励的在线微调策略，仅用50条真实轨迹即可将预训练模型适配到全新环境。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真的可控未来帧，但经常产生幻觉——视觉流畅但与真实动态偏离。作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，并提出轻量级数据驱动信号既可检测幻觉又可指导缓解。

Method: 1) 构建MMBench2数据集（427小时、210任务）并训练350M参数世界模型；2) 识别三种幻觉模式：感知幻觉、动作边缘化幻觉、场景发散幻觉；3) 开发三种预测信号来准确预测模型失败位置；4) 训练阶段采用覆盖率感知采样技术；5) 在线阶段将幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励，引导针对性数据收集，实现数据高效的微调。

Result: 三种幻觉模式被明确定义和定位。所提出的幻觉预测信号能准确预测模型失败位置。覆盖率感知采样和基于好奇心奖励的微调策略仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适配到全新未见环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是一个数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的信号同样可以用于缓解幻觉。

Original Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

中文摘要翻译: 现代生成式世界模型能够渲染出越来越逼真且动作可控的未来帧，但它们经常产生幻觉：生成的序列在视觉上依然流畅，却偏离了真实的动态变化。我们假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，在这种区域中，轻量级的数据驱动信号既可以检测幻觉，也可以指导缓解措施。为验证这一假设，我们构建了MMBench2——一个包含427小时、210个任务的数据集，用于视觉世界建模，配备真实动作、奖励和实时模拟器，并在此数据集上训练了一个350M参数的世界模型。我们识别出三种不同的幻觉模式：感知幻觉、动作边缘化幻觉和场景发散幻觉——每种模式对应流水线的不同阶段，并开发了三种能准确预测模型失败位置的信号。为在训练时缩小覆盖缺口，我们提出了一种覆盖率感知的采样技术；为在线缩小覆盖缺口，我们将幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励用于针对性数据收集，从而得到一种数据高效的微调方法，仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适配到完全未见的环境中。总体而言，我们的发现揭示出世界模型中的幻觉本质上是一个数据覆盖问题，并且用于检测幻觉的信号同样可以用于缓解幻觉。

</details>


### [189] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为误差条件神经求解器（ENS）的新方法，通过将PDE残差场作为网络输入而非优化目标，让网络学习迭代修正预测的策略，在四个PDE系列中取得了最高预测精度，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将求解视为纯统计任务，无法纠正约束违反和外推；混合方法通过梯度下降最小化PDE残差，但计算成本高且不稳定。作者从理论和实验上证明了数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能不可靠，无法保证预测精度。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），将PDE残差场作为网络的直接输入（而非优化目标），使网络能够读取自身误差的空间结构，并学习一种迭代更新策略来逐步修正预测。

Result: 在四个PDE系列中，ENS在绝大多数设置下取得了最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上增益达10倍；同时避免了混合方法的高计算成本。ENS的学习修正策略在分布偏移下具有泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移，在病态区域优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中不是重建精度的可靠代理指标；将残差作为输入而非优化目标，让网络学习迭代修正策略，能同时实现高精度、低计算成本和良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 神经代理模型能够提供从PDE参数到解的快速近似映射，但它们通常将求解视为纯粹的统计任务：一旦训练完成，它们很难纠正自身的约束违反行为，也无法外推到训练分布之外。最近的混合方法通过梯度下降或高斯-牛顿步来针对PDE残差以提升物理正确性，但继承了底层经典优化器的高计算成本和不稳定性。我们从理论和实验上证明，在病态系统中，数值最小化PDE残差可能是重建精度的不可靠代理指标，这解释了为什么这些方法尽管实现了低残差，却常常无法做出准确预测。我们提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），它基于一个不同的原理：将PDE残差场作为网络的直接输入（而非优化目标），使网络能够在每次迭代中读取自身误差的空间结构，并学习一种更新策略来迭代地修正其预测。在四个PDE系列上，ENS在绝大多数设置下取得了最高的预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上增益达到10倍，同时避免了混合方法的高计算成本。ENS学习到的修正策略在分布偏移下具有泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移，其中在残差最小化最不可靠的病态区域，其相对优势最大。项目网站：https://neuralsolver.github.io/。

</details>


### [190] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），一种基于自回归建模而非归一化流的新框架，用于高效采样分子系统的热力学平衡态，在多个基准测试上显著优于基于流的模型，并在大规模可迁移模型Robin上取得了超过60%的零样本能量误差降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的玻尔兹曼生成器（BG）主要依赖归一化流（NF），但NF存在严格可逆性约束导致的表达力受限（离散时间）或计算成本高昂的似然（连续时间）问题，限制了其在大分子系统上的可扩展性和性能。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模框架替代基于流的BG范式，绕过了流的拓扑约束，支持推理时序列干预，并利用在大语言模型中有效的架构增强可扩展性。

Result: ArBG在所有基准测试上均显著优于基于流的模型，特别是在更大肽系统（如10残基的Chignolin）上表现突出。此外，基于ArBG框架训练的132M参数可迁移模型Robin，在8残基系统上将零样本能量误差E-W₂降低了超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归建模是替代归一化流构建玻尔兹曼生成器的一种有效且可扩展的新范式，能够克服流的固有局限性，在分子平衡态采样任务上取得显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

中文摘要翻译: 在热力学平衡态下高效采样分子系统是统计物理学中的一个标志性挑战。这一挑战推动了玻尔兹曼生成器（BG）的发展，该类生成器通过结合生成模型、精确似然以及重要性采样校正，能够快速生成无关联的平衡态样本。然而，现代BG主要依赖归一化流（NF），后者要么因严格的可逆性约束而表达力受限（离散时间），要么因计算代价高昂的似然而受限（连续时间）。本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG）——一种新颖的自回归建模框架——通过摆脱基于流的BG范式来克服这些局限性。ArBG规避了流的拓扑约束，支持推理时的序列干预，并通过利用在大语言模型中高效的架构增强了可扩展性。我们通过实验证明，ArBG在所有基准测试上均显著优于基于流的模型，尤其是在更大的肽系统（如10残基的Chignolin）上。此外，我们提出了Robin，这是一个基于ArBG框架训练的1.32亿参数可迁移模型，该模型相较之前的最先进方法有了显著改进，在8残基系统上将零样本能量误差E-W₂降低了超过60%。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg。

</details>


### [191] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励信号（执行反馈）训练LLM，在没有真实解的情况下也能提升代码生成能力，并在精确解基准上取得迁移效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖真实答案作为奖励信号，限制了其在没有标准解的场景下的应用。论文旨在探索如何利用基于分数的优化任务（无真实解）来训练LLM，并克服连续奖励带来的尺度支配和频率支配问题。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 校准奖励塑造（calibrated reward shaping），通过实例级比较消除未校准分数带来的尺度支配；(2) 强调排名靠前的求解器，同时对其他有效解保留有界反馈，以缓解频率支配问题。采用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督信号，结合组相对强化学习进行训练。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414分别提升8.9%和9.4%的ALE排名。更关键的是，仅使用分数任务训练，RiVER在LiveCodeBench和USACO等精确解基准上分别提升2.4%和3.5%的绝对平均改进，而使用原始执行分数的基线无法迁移到精确解基准。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在没有标准解的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境；RiVER不仅提升了分数任务表现，还成功迁移到精确解任务，证明了无真实解训练范式的可行性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在训练大语言模型时通常依赖于真实答案来分配奖励，这限制了其在真实解未知的任务中的适用性。本文提出了一种排名诱导的可验证框架（RiVER），该框架在没有真实解的情况下，利用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督信号，在基于分数的优化任务上训练大语言模型。当将组相对强化学习应用于此类连续奖励时，我们识别出两个关键挑战：尺度支配——未校准的分数幅度在不同测试实例间扭曲策略更新；以及频率支配——反复采样的次优解可能掩盖罕见的更强候选解。RiVER通过校准的奖励塑造来解决这些挑战，该方法使用实例级比较并强调排名靠前的求解器，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。我们在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上进行训练，并在算法工程基准（ALE-Bench）、LiveCodeBench和USACO上评估。RiVER使Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE排名上分别提升了8.9%和9.4%。更重要的是，尽管仅在没有真实解的分数任务上训练，RiVER在LiveCodeBench和USACO等精确解基准上相较于基础模型取得了2.4%和3.5%的绝对平均改进。相比之下，使用原始执行分数训练的基线模型提升了ALE排名，但未能迁移到精确解基准。这些结果表明，基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在没有真实解的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境。

</details>
